1b1('dul ba): @#//rgya gar skad du/bi na ya k+tu dra bstu/bod skad du/'dul ba phran tshegs kyi gzhi/bam po dang po/thams cad mkhyen pa la phyag 'tshal lo//phran 
1b2('dul ba): tshegs kyi gzhi'i sdom ni/so phag chen po chos gos dang //shing tshad dang ni gnas brtan lus//rgyus pa 'gyod pa dbyung ba nyid//phran tshegs gzhir ni yang dag bshad//
1b3('dul ba): bsdus pa'i sngon ni/so phag sen mo bcos pa dang //lhung bzed yan lag me long skyes//lam phran chu tshugs mos sran sde'u//rkang pa kru dang gos mdud do//sdom ni 
1b4('dul ba): so phag dang ni sa gar dang /nya phyis dri yi lag ris dang //ka ba dang ni skud pa gnyis//lag rgyan dang ni sor gdu brgya//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das yangs pa can na 
1b5('dul ba): spre'u rdzing gi 'gram khang pa na bzhug so//drug sde rnams snga dro sha ma thabs dang chos gos bgos dang lhung bzed thogs te/yangs pa can du bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs pa dang /
1b6('dul ba): yangs pa can gyi bar dang /khang pa brtsegs pa'i bar gyi bar skabs de na yangs pa can gyi ling tshe byi rnams kyi bskyed mos tshal me tog dang /'bras bu dang /chu phun sum 
1b7('dul ba): tshogs pa/bya sna tshogs skad 'byin pa dga' ba'i tshal dang 'dra ba de dag gi brtsegs pa dang /gar dang glu dang /rol mo'i sgra'i chu byad sna tshogs dag dang /khrus kyi yo 
1b8('dul ba): byad sna tshogs dag kyang bzhag pa dang /drug sde rnams kyis smras pa/dka' bo nye dga' bo/yangs pa can gyi ling tsha byi 'di dag dang /bskyed mos tshal gyi dga' ba ni 
2a1('dul ba): @#//bcom ldan 'das kyis sum bcu rtsa gsum pa'i lha rnams kyi ngo bor mdzad na/re shig de dag gi bskyed mos tshal ci 'dra ba blta'o zhes de rnams glong nas de dag gi bskyed mos tsha la du bzhugs so//de dag gis de na brtsal ba'i yo byad mnga' bo che dang drang ba dang 
2a2('dul ba): 'phar rdo sna tshogs dang /gar dang glu dang /rol mo'i sgra'i cha byad sna tshogs dang /bi pang dang /gling bu dang /rdza rnga dang /khar rnga dang /skad snyan la sogs pa sna tshogs dang /khrus kyi yo byad so phag dang /bkru ba'i phye ma dang /mgo bkru ba skyu ru ra sna tshogs mthong ba dang /de rnams 
2a3('dul ba): kyis phan tshun smras pa/dga'o nye dga' bo yo byad phun sum tshogs pa rnam pa du ma 'di dag yod na/ci re shig brtsal pa bya 'am/'on te gar bya 'am/glu blang ngam/rol mo'i sgra bya 'am/khrus bya/kha cig gis smras pa/bdag cig bkrus nas ring zhig lon gyis 
2a4('dul ba): de khrus bya'o zhes de dag glong nas so phag dag gis drung cing drung nas khrus bya bar brtsams pa dang /bzo'i gnas dang /las kyi gnas de dag la mkhas pas khrus la zhugs pa de rnams kyi sgra yid du 'ong ba 'di lta ste/sil snyan yan lag lnga pa skyes bu mkhas pas legs par dkrol ba'i lta bu 
2a5('dul ba): byung ngo //skye bo'i tshogs phal po che lam de nas 'gro bas sgra de thos pa dang /de rnams rna bya ste/yud tsam bsdad nas smras pa/shes ldan dag yangs pa can gyi ling tsha byi rnams kyi bskyed mos tshal na gar mkhan rnams gar byed pa ltar 'dod dam/de ltar bya'o zhes ltad mo la yid 
2a6('dul ba): 'dun pa de dag myur bar chas te bskyed mos tshal du dod nas de dag nang du zhugs pa dang /drug sde rnams kyang phyir byung ngo //de dag gis de rnams la smras pa/'phags pa dag phyogs gang na gar mkhan rnams gar bgyid/rna de rnams kyis smras pa/bkren pa khyed kyis bye phrag mi phye de de/
2a7('dul ba): gar mkhan gar byed pa la sgra 'di lta bu 'byung ngam/'phags pa dga'o na 'di su'i sgra lags/shes ldan dag kho bo cag lus drung nas khrus byas pa yin no//'phags pa dag ci dge sbyong khyed 'dod pa'i yon tan gyis gzir tam/bkren pa khyed kyi mgrin pa rdog pas mnan 
2a8('dul ba): la dge sbyong kho bo cag 'dod pa'i yon tan gyis bzir to//khyed kho bo cag gi rjes su ston pa yin nam ci/ma smra bar deng /khyed la gnod pa byas ser zhes de rnams kyis de dag bsdigs pa dang /spa gong nas/de de ste/yangs pa can du zhugs nas lam po che dang /srang dang /gzhi mdo dang /
2b1('dul ba): dang /sum mdo dag tu 'phya bar brtsams so//dge slong rnams kyis bskabs de thos nas bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis dgongs pa/nyes dmigs gang cung zad byung ba ni dge slong rnams so phag dag gis lus drung de khrus byed 
2b2('dul ba): pa las gyur gyis/de lta bas na/dge slong gis so phag gis//lus drung de khrus mi bya'o//'drung na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong gis so phag gis lus drung de khrus mi bya'o zhes bka' stsal pa dang /dge slong dag rkang pa se rag 
2b3('dul ba): byung zhing dri ma dang bcas bzhin bsod snyoms la 'jug pa de dag bram ze dang /khyim bdag rnams kyis mthong nas smras pa/'phags pa dag khyed zhabs se rag byung zhing dri ma dang bcas na/ci ste dri ma sbyang bar mi bgyi/de rnams kyis smras pa/bzhin bzang ba dag bcom 
2b4('dul ba): ldan 'das kyis ma gnad do//ci 'phags pa khyer dri ma'i 'dam bcangs pas dag par 'gyur ram/de rnams cad mi smra bar 'dug ste/bsod snyoms la brgyus nas gtsug lag khang du dong ba dang /skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la rgyas par gsol 
2b5('dul ba): te/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag de ltar na snga min bcas pa'o//'di ni gnang ba yin te/de lta bas na dge slong gis rkang pa so phag gis drung bar gnang ngo //lus gzhan ni ma min te/'drud na 'gal cha bas can du 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 
2b6('dul ba): 'das yangs pa can na spre'u rdzing gi 'gram khang pa brtsegs pa na zhugs so//dge slong rnams snga dro sham thabs dang chos gos bgos/lhung bzed thogs te yangs pa can du bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs pa dang /de rnams kyis bram ze zhig sa dkar gyis so 
2b7('dul ba): ris gsum byugs nas slongs mo la rgyu zhing de'i bslangs pa yang mang du rnyed par mthong de//drug sde rnams kyis smras pa/dga' bo nye dga' bo thabs 'di ni bzang po zhig ste/bdag cag kyang de ltar bya'o zhes de rnams phyi de nyin sa dkar gyis so ris 
2b8('dul ba): gsum byugs nas bsod snyoms la zhugs pa ma dad pa rnams kyis mthong nas 'phya zhing gus par smra'o//gus par smra'o zhes smras pa dang /drug sde rnams kyis smras pa bkren pa khyed kyis gus par smras par bya ba gang dag yin pa dang /phyag bye ba gang 
3a1('dul ba): @#//dag yin pa 'di yang mi shes sam/de dag gis smras pa/'di la ci zhig 'tshal bar bgyi/bram ze rgan pa rgan pa rnams ni gus par smra bar bya ba yin la/dge slong rnams ni phyag bya ba yin na/de ci'i phyir khyed dge slong rnams la gus par smra ba byed kyi 
3a2('dul ba): phyag ma yin/'phags pa dag bdag cag gis khyed bram ze lags par 'tshal gyi dge slong du ni ma lags so//de lta lags pas bdag cag gis ma 'tshal gyis bzod par gsol lo zhes byas pa dang /de dag cang mi smra bar 'dug go/skabs de dge slong rnams kyis thos nas 
3a3('dul ba): bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis dgongs pa/nyes dmigs gang cung zad ci byung ba ni dge slong rnams so ris gsum 'dri ba las gyur gyis/de lta bas na dge slong gis sor ris gsum bri ba mi bya'o//dge slong sor ris gsum 'dri na 'gal tshabs can du 
3a4('dul ba): 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong gis sor ris gsam mi 'byug go zhes bka' stsal pa dang /dge slong dag sman pas bsgo yang lus sas mi skud pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyi bcom ldan 'das la gsol to//bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag de ltar 
3a5('dul ba): na snga ma ni bcas pa'o//'di ni gnang ba yin te/de lta bas na sman pas bsgo ba ni ma gtogs so//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod na rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' rab na bzhugs so//drug sde rnams snga dro sham thabs dang chos gos bgos/lhung 
3a6('dul ba): bzed thogs te mnyan yod du bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs pa na/de rnams kyis bram ze 'ge'i wang gi thig le byas pa zhig skye bo phal po che'i tshogs kyis bskor zhing ston mo'i bsngags pa brjod pa mthong ba dang /de rnams kyis smras pa/dga' bo nye dga' bo thabs 'di ni bzang po zhig ste/bdag 
3a7('dul ba): cag kyang 'di ltar bya'o//legs kyis de ltar bya'o zhes de rnams phyi de nyin 'ga'i wang gi thig le dag gis bdag nyid brgyan pa byas nas bsod snyoms la zhugs pa dang /bram ze dang khyim bdag dag gis mthong nas 'phya zhing gus par smra'o gus par smra'o//zhes smras pa dang /drug 
3a8('dul ba): sde rnams kyis smras pa/bkren pa khyed kyis gus par smra bar bya ba gang dag yin pa dang /phyag bya ba gang dag yin pa 'di yang mi shes sam/de dag gis smras pa/'di la ci zhig 'tshal bar bgyi/bram ze rgan pa rgan pa rnams ni gus par smra bar bya ba yin la/dge slong rnams ni phyag 
3b1('dul ba): bya ba yin na/de ci'i phyir zhe na/khyed dge slong rnams la gus par smra ba byed kyi phyag ma yin/'phags pa dag bdag cag gis khyed bram ze lags par 'tshal gyi dge slong du ni ma lags so//de'i slad du bdag cag gis ma 'tshal gyis bzod par gsol lo zhes bya sa 
3b2('dul ba): pa dang /de dag cad mi smra bar 'dug go/skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis dgongs pa/nyes dmigs gang cung zad ni byung ba ni dge slong dag 'gi'u wang gi thig le dag gis bdag nyid brgyan pa las gyur gyis/de lta 
3b3('dul ba): bas na dge slong gis 'g-i'u wang gi thig le dag gi brgyan par mi bya'o//brgyan na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong gis 'gi'u wang gi thig le dag/gis bdag nyid brgyan bar mi bya'o zhes bka' stsal pa dang /dge slong zhig dpral bar rma byung ba de sman 
3b4('dul ba): pa'i drung du song nas bzhin bzangs bdag la sman ston cig/'phags pa rma'i than kor 'gi'u wang gis skus shig dang khyod 'tsho bar 'gyur ro//des smras pa/bcom ldan 'das kyis ma gnang ngo //'phags pa khyod kyi ston pa ni thugs rje can yin pas gnang bar 'gyur ro//
3b5('dul ba): skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/de lta bas na dge slong dag sman pas bsgo ba ni ma gtogs so//bcom ldan 'das kyis de dge slong gis so phag gis lus drung bar mi bya'o zhes bka' 
3b6('dul ba): stsal pa dang /dge slong rnams nya phyis kyis 'drud par byed do//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka'-i stsal pa/nya phyis kyis 'drung bar mi bya'o//'drung nas 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod na rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal mgon med zas sbyin 
3b7('dul ba): gyi kun dga' ra ba na bzhugs so//drug sde nams dris byugs te/gzhon nu rnams kyi nang du dong nas smras pa/gzhon nu dag dad kyi dri ji lta snoms shig/de dag gis smras pa/gnas brtan ci khyed dris byugs sam/byugs so'o//gnas brtan khyed dris byug tu rung ngam/
3b8('dul ba): rung yang rung mi rung yang rung ste/de yang ded kyis ni byugs so//de dag 'phye smod kha zer ba'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis dgongs pa/nyes dmigs gang cung zad byung ba ni dris nyug pa las gyur gyis de lta bas na 
4a1('dul ba): dge slong rnams kyis dris byug par mi bya'o//nyug na 'gal tsha bas can du 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rnams kyis dri byug par mi bya'o zhes bka' stsal pa dang /dge slong na nga bzhig sman pa'i drung du song ste/bzhin bzangs bdag la sman ston pa cig 
4a2('dul ba): des smras pa/'phags pa dris byugs na khyod 'tsho bar 'gyur ro//bzhin bzangs ci kho bo 'dod pa spyod dam/'phags pa khyod kyi sman ni 'di yin te/gzhan gyis ni 'tsho bar mi nus so//skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom 
4a3('dul ba): ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/de lta bas na gnang gis sman pa'i lung gis dri bcad par bya'o//dge slong dag dris byugs nas dge 'dun gyi nang na 'dug cing bram ze dang khyim bdag 'ongs shing lhags pa rnams la chos ston par byed/bram ze dang khyim bdag gi khyim rnams su 
4a4('dul ba): yang 'gro bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyi bcom ldan 'das la gsolab dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsalapa/ngas dge slong dri 'chang ba'i kun tu spyad pa'i chos bca' bar bya ste/dge slong dri 'chang bas dris byugs nas dge 'du gyi nang du 'dug par mi bya/
4a5('dul ba): bram ze dang khyim bdag longs shing lhags pa rnams chos ston par mi bya/bram ze dang khyim bdagag gi khyim rnams su 'gro bar mi bya'i/na ma sos par gyur bde'i tshe khrus byas nas dri med par gyur pa dang dge 'dun gyi nang du 'dug par bya/bram ze dang khyim pa dag 'ongs shing 
4a6('dul ba): lhags pa rnams la chos bstan par bya/bram ze dang khyi ma bdag gis khyim rnams su 'gro bar bya'o//dge slong dri 'chang bas kun du spyad pa'i chos ji ltar bcas pa bzhin yang dag par blangs te mi 'dug na 'gal tsha pas can du 'gyur ro//bram ze dang khyim bdag dang bcan 
4a7('dul ba): dag dge slong rnams la dri 'bul ba na/dge slong rnams 'gyod nas mi len pa dang de dag gis smras pa/'phags pa dag gang gi tshe sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'jig rten du byung bar ma gyur pa de'i tshe na mu stegs can rnams yon gnas lags/da ni bcom ldan 'das 'jig 
4a8('dul ba): rten du gyur bas da ltar khyed yon gnas lags na/gal te khyed kyis ma bzhengs na ci bdag cag 'jig rten 'di nas 'jig rten pha rol tu dge ba'i lam rgyags ma mchis par 'chi 'am/bdag cag la thugs brtse ba'i slad du bzhes shig/de dag gis smras pa/bcom ldan 
4b1('dul ba): 'das la zhu'o//skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/blang bar bya'o//dge slong rnams kyis blangs nas/de dag nyid kyi mdun du bor ba dang /de dag gis smras pa/'phags pa dag bdag cag gis 
4b2('dul ba): rin thang bzhin du nyo nyon khyed kyis blangs te dor ram/skabs de dge slong rnams kyi bcom ldan 'das la gsol/ba dang /bcom ldan 'dasakyis bka' stsal pa/blangs nas dor ba mi bya'i/'on kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod rten la dri' lag ris byug par bya'o zhes 
4b3('dul ba): bka' stsal pa dang /dge slong rnams kyis blang nas dbu skra dang sen mo'i mchod rten dag la dri'i lag ris nyug go/de dag gis smras pa/'phag sa pa dag ci bdag cag gis mchod rten ma mthong ngam/bdag cag gis ni khyed kho na la phul ste/dbu skra dang sen mo'i mcho da rten 
4b4('dul ba): dag la ni sngar phul lags so//skabs da dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/blang nas gnas khang la byug par bya'o//de rnams kyis gnas khang gi sgo la byugs pa dang /de dag dri gtsang khang yin 
4b5('dul ba): no snyam nas phyag 'tshal bar byed pa'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom/ldan 'das la gsol ba dang/bcom ldan 'das kyis bga' stsal pa/nang du byug par bya'o//de slong rnams kyis drang thang tu byugs pa dang/nyes dmigs de nyid du gyur nas bcom ldan 'das
4b6('dul ba): kyis bka' stsal pa/sngas dang nye ba' markang ba dang nye bar byug par bya zhing dri zhim po thams cad mig la phan pas dus dus su bsnam par bya ste/'di la 'gyod par mi bya'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das chu srin byis pa bsod kyang la 'jigs 'jigs lta'i tshal ri dgas kyi
4b7('dul ba): nags na bzhugs so//ji tsam na rgyal bu gzhon nu rtogs byed kyi khang bzangs bad ma ris byed du bcug nas bcom ldan 'das nyan thos kyi dge 'dun dang bcas pa gdugs tshod la spyan drangs ste/bcom ldan 'das khang bzangs bang ma'i ris su dge slong gi dge 'dun gyi gung la
4b8('dul ba): nga ma gshams pa la zhugs so//nye dga' bzon za ba'i tshe lag pas ka ba la bsnun pa dang/khang bzangs pad ma ris g.yos bar gyur to//'dri ma mkhan rnams kyis smras pa/'phags brgyal bu rtogs byed kyis khang bzangs pad ma ris tshon brgya rtsa gcig gis/
5a1('dul ba): @#//bkra bar byas pa khyed kyis gzhig gam ci/nye dga' bos smras pa/bkren pa rtogs byed 'di la lhag par chags pas dus byas nas gar skye gtol med do//khyed kyang 'di la lhag par chags pa sa dus byas nas yi dgas wa ba can rnams kyi nang 
5a2('dul ba): du skye bar 'gyur ro//de dag kha zer ba'i skabs de dge slong rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis dgongs pa nyes dmigs gang cung zad byung ba ni dge slong dag ka ba la lag pas bsnun pa las gyur gyis/de lta bas na dge slong gis 
5a3('dul ba): lag pas ka ba la bsnun par mi bya'o//bsnun na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong gis ka ba la lag pas bsnunapar mi bya zhes bka' stsal pa dang /drug sde rnams khu tshur dang gru mo dang /phrag pa dang /mgo dang /rtsib logs dang /rgyab 
5a4('dul ba): dang /keng pa dang /brla dang /bus mo dang /byin pa dang /rkang pa dang /dbyug pa dang /bong ba dang /gzhan gyis bsnun par byed pas nyes dmigs de nyid du gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/gang gis kyang bsnun par mi bya'o//drug sde rnams cig pa dang 
5a5('dul ba): sa dang da gzhan la bsnun par byed pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' stsal pa/gzhan la yang gang dag gis kyang bsnun par mi bye'o//bsnun na 'gal tsha bas can du 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod na rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi 'tshal 
5a6('dul ba): mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' rab na bzhugs so//drug sde rnams snga dro mnyan yod du bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs pa dang /de rnams kyis bram ze zhig tshangs pa'i skud pa thogs te slongs mo la rgyu zhing des bza' ba dang bca' ba yang mang du rnyed pa mthong nas/dug sde rnams 
5a7('dul ba): kyis smras pa/dga' bo nye dga' ba thabs 'di ni bzang po zhig ste/bdag cag kyang de lta bu bya'o zhes de dag phyi de nyin tshangs pa'i skud pa thogs ste/bsod snyoms la zhugs pa ma dad pa rnams kyis mthong nas/'phya zhi dgus par smra'o//gus par smra'o zhes smras pa dang 
5a8('dul ba): drag sde rnams kyis smras pa/bkren pa khyed kyis gus par smra bar bya ba gang dag yin pa dang /phyag bya ba gang dag yin pa 'di yang mi shes sam/de dag gis smras pa/'di la ci zhig 'tshal par bgyi/bram ze rgan pa rgan pa rnams ni gus par smra bar bya ba yin la/dge slong 
5b1('dul ba): rnams ni phyag bya ba yin na/de ci'i phyir khyed dge slong rnams la gus par smra bar byed kyi phyag ma yin/'phags pa dag bdag cag gis khyed bram ze lags par 'tshal gyi dge slong du ni ma lags so//de lta lags pas/bdag cag gis ma 'tshal gyis bzod par gsol ba lo zhes 
5b2('dul ba): byas pa dang /de dag cang mi smra bar 'dug go/skabs de dge slong rnams kyis thos nas bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis dgongs pa/nyes dmigs gang cung zad ci byung ba ni dge slong rnams tshangs pa'i skud pa thogs pa las gyur gyis/de lta bas na dge slong 
5b3('dul ba): gis tshangs pa'i skud pa gdags par mi bya'o//'dogs na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das mnyan yod na rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' rab na bzhugs so//drug sde rnams snga dro sham thabs dang chos gos 
5b4('dul ba): bgos/lhung bzed thogs te mnyan yod tu bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs pa na/de dag gis bram ze dri dang me tog gis brgyan pa tshon/sna snga ba'i skud pas lag pa bcings pa zhig dus ston zos nas dbang po tshim ste/khyim nas byung ba mthong ba dang /de dag gis smras pa/
5b5('dul ba): dga' bo nyi dga' bo thabs 'di ni bzang po zhig ste/bdag cag kyang 'di bzhin bya'o zhes de dag phye de nyin tshon sna lnga pa'i skud pas lag pa bcing nas bsod snyoms la zhugs pa/bram ze dang khyim bdag rnams kyis mthong nas 'phya zhing gus par smra'o//gus par smra'o zhes 
5b6('dul ba): smras pa dang /drug sdes rnams kyis smras pa/bkren pa khyed kyis gus par smra bar bya ba gang dag yin pa dang /phyag bya ba gang dag yin pa 'di yang mi shes sam/de dag gis smras pa/'di la ci zhig 'tshal bar bgyi/bram ze rgan ba rgan pa rnams ni gus par smra bar bya ba yin pa 
5b7('dul ba): la/dge slong rnams ni phyag bye ba yin na/de ci'i phyir khyed dge slong rnams la gus par smra bar byed kyi phyag ma yin/'phags pa dag bdag cag gis khyed bram ze lags par 'tshal gyi/dge slong du ni ma lags so//de lta lags pas bdag cag gis

11a1('dul ba): gsungs nas bsod snyoms bzhes te gshegs so//de dang ha cang mi ring ba zhig na gcer bu ba zhig 'dug pa des bsams pa/gang bdag cag la bslad bi ba'i khyim gcig bu zhig yod pa de yang dge sbyong gau ta mas kha drangs kyis
